; New C2686C Decompressed Block via New Title Screen ips
; Reuses Japanese title screen code, I think
; Mostly duplicative of US version, but with many different offsets
; [;] -> Line not yet explored
; [ ] -> Line is practically identical to US version
; [-] -> Line from US version is not present
; [+] -> Line is not present in US version
; [x] -> Line is changed in a meaningful way
  

  $7E/5000 4C 0C 50    JMP $500C  ; Title Screen
  $7E/5003 4C A7 57    JMP $57A7  ; Opening Credits
  $7E/5006 4C 50 69    JMP $6950  ; Rise of the Floating Continent
  $7E/5009 4C 7E 6C    JMP $6C7E  ; World being torn apart
